** cmnd= airflow-sbom.pcs -i sbom_fullUpdate
** cmnd= apt list airflowAcctEnvSetup --installed  2> /dev/null
Listing...
** cmnd= 
# 1. Dedicated system user -- only when absent, useradd exits 9 otherwise
id airflow >/dev/null 2>&1 || sudo useradd -r -m -s /bin/bash airflow

# 2. AIRFLOW_HOME and the dirs airflow must own. No venv here -- see docstring.
sudo mkdir -p /opt/airflow/dags /opt/airflow/logs
sudo chown -R airflow:airflow /opt/airflow

# 3. tmpfiles daemon configuration for PID and socket files
echo "D /run/airflow 0755 airflow airflow -" | sudo tee /etc/tmpfiles.d/airflow.conf >/dev/null
sudo systemd-tmpfiles --create

# 4. EnvironmentFile for the airflow-*.service units. Written only when absent,
#    so hand-added overrides survive re-runs. systemd reads it as root before
#    dropping to User=airflow, so root:airflow 640 suffices: readable by the
#    service account, not writable by it.
# The content, and why BASE_URL and EXECUTION_API_SERVER_URL matter, is in
# airflowInfo.envFileContent().
if [ ! -f /etc/default/airflow ] ; then
    sudo tee /etc/default/airflow >/dev/null <<'AIRFLOW_ENV_EOF'
AIRFLOW_HOME=/opt/airflow
AIRFLOW__DATABASE__SQL_ALCHEMY_CONN=postgresql+psycopg2://@/airflow?host=/var/run/postgresql
AIRFLOW__API__BASE_URL=http://airflow.internal
AIRFLOW__CORE__EXECUTION_API_SERVER_URL=http://localhost:24006/execution/
# Add any overrides or environment variables needed by your DAGs here
AIRFLOW_ENV_EOF
fi
sudo chown root:airflow /etc/default/airflow
sudo chmod 640 /etc/default/airflow

** cmnd= apt list airflowPostgresSetup --installed  2> /dev/null
Listing...
** cmnd= 
# 1. Cluster must be running. Debian's postgresql-common starts it at install
#    time --- except under policy-rc.d (containers), where it silently does not.
#    Version/cluster are discovered, not hardcoded, so a major-version bump of
#    the postgresql metapackage does not strand this.
sudo systemctl enable postgresql >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
read PGVER PGCLUSTER <<< "$(pg_lsclusters -h | awk 'NR==1 {print $1, $2}')"
sudo pg_ctlcluster "$PGVER" "$PGCLUSTER" start 2>/dev/null || true

# 2. LOGIN role named for the OS account -- peer auth maps one to the other.
sudo -u postgres psql -tAc "SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='airflow'" \
    | grep -q 1 || sudo -u postgres psql -c "CREATE ROLE airflow LOGIN"

# 3. Database owned by that role, so airflow db migrate can create its schema.
sudo -u postgres psql -tAc "SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname='airflow'" \
    | grep -q 1 || sudo -u postgres createdb -O airflow airflow

# 4. Five airflow units each hold a SQLAlchemy pool; stock max_connections=100
#    is tight. ALTER SYSTEM needs a restart to take effect, so only do both
#    when the value is actually low --- otherwise every sbom run bounces the DB.
PGMAXCONN=$(sudo -u postgres psql -tAc "show max_connections")
if [ "$PGMAXCONN" -lt 200 ] ; then
    sudo -u postgres psql -c "ALTER SYSTEM SET max_connections = 200" >/dev/null
    sudo pg_ctlcluster "$PGVER" "$PGCLUSTER" restart
fi

CREATE ROLE

asRootWrite: destPath=/etc/systemd/system/airflow-db.service -- contentStr=
[Unit]
Description=Airflow DB Migration
# The metadata DB is PostgreSQL (see airflow-sbom.pcs airflowPostgresSetup).
# This is the only unit that needs to name it: airflow-db is the first thing
# to touch the database, and the four long-running units all carry
# Requires=/After=airflow-db.service, so ordering reaches them transitively
# -- airflow-db is Type=oneshot+RemainAfterExit, so "after airflow-db" means
# after db migrate has completed, which is necessarily after postgres is up.
After=network.target postgresql.service
Requires=postgresql.service

[Service]
Type=oneshot
RemainAfterExit=yes
User=airflow
Group=airflow
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/airflow
ExecStart=/bisos/venv/py3/asc/bin/airflow db migrate

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
 seedtype=sysdSysUnit
** cmnd= sudo systemctl daemon-reload
** cmnd= sudo systemctl restart airflow-db.service
** cmnd= sudo systemctl enable airflow-db.service
** cmnd= airflow-webserver-sysd.pcs -i sysdSysUnit ensure
Action ensure
** cmnd= /bisos/venv/py3/bisos3/bin/airflow-webserver-sysd.pcs  --cls="configFile_sysdUnit" --runAs="root"  -i configFileUpdate
asRootWrite: destPath=/etc/systemd/system/airflow-webserver.service -- contentStr=
[Unit]
Description=Airflow API Server
After=network.target airflow-db.service
Requires=airflow-db.service

[Service]
User=airflow
Group=airflow
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/airflow
ExecStart=/bisos/venv/py3/asc/bin/airflow api-server --port 24006
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
 seedtype=sysdSysUnit
** cmnd= sudo systemctl daemon-reload

** cmnd= apt list kafkaAcctSetup --installed  2> /dev/null
Listing...
** cmnd= 
set -e
# 1. System account with its banna-registered uid/gid, no login shell

# banna sysAccts: kafka uid=503, group kafka gid=503
if getent group kafka >/dev/null ; then
    liveGid=$(getent group kafka | cut -d: -f3)
    [ "$liveGid" = "503" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: group kafka has gid $liveGid, banna assigns 503 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent group 503 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: gid 503 (registered for group kafka) is held by group $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo groupadd -r -g 503 kafka
fi
if getent passwd kafka >/dev/null ; then
    liveUid=$(id -u kafka)
    [ "$liveUid" = "503" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: account kafka has uid $liveUid, banna assigns 503 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent passwd 503 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: uid 503 (registered for kafka) is held by account $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo useradd -r -u 503 -g kafka -M -d /var/lib/kafka \
        -s /usr/sbin/nologin -c 'Apache Kafka and Kafbat UI (bisos.kafka)' kafka
fi


# 2. Data and application-log dirs belong to the account. Binaries do not (see kafkaDistSetup).
sudo mkdir -p /var/lib/kafka/data /var/log/kafka /etc/kafka /opt/kafka
sudo chown -R kafka:kafka /var/lib/kafka /var/log/kafka
sudo chmod 750 /var/lib/kafka/data

** cmnd= apt list kafkaDistSetup --installed  2> /dev/null
Listing...
** cmnd= 
set -e
if [ ! -x /opt/kafka/kafka_2.13-4.3.1/bin/kafka-server-start.sh ] ; then
    tmpDir=$(mktemp -d)
    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpDir"' EXIT
    curl -fsSL -o "$tmpDir/kafka_2.13-4.3.1.tgz" https://archive.apache.org/dist/kafka/4.3.1/kafka_2.13-4.3.1.tgz
    echo "c7d7b2318cb51aa0c61d3246a51c349210073c5c9b754947ef965a439f2f939e8600f204e134a75ac31faf3829c9370960ef7c6a9886c8a1dbf0339a21f4c54c  $tmpDir/kafka_2.13-4.3.1.tgz" | sha512sum -c --quiet -
    sudo tar -xzf "$tmpDir/kafka_2.13-4.3.1.tgz" -C /opt/kafka
    sudo chown -R root:root /opt/kafka/kafka_2.13-4.3.1
fi

# kafka-run-class.sh mkdirs $LOG_DIR, defaulting to <dist>/logs, for every tool
# invocation. Pointing that at the account-owned logDir keeps the dist read-only.
[ -e /opt/kafka/kafka_2.13-4.3.1/logs ] || sudo ln -s /var/log/kafka /opt/kafka/kafka_2.13-4.3.1/logs

sudo ln -sfn /opt/kafka/kafka_2.13-4.3.1 /opt/kafka/current

** cmnd= apt list kafkaConfSetup --installed  2> /dev/null
Listing...
** cmnd= 
set -e
if [ ! -f /etc/kafka/server.properties ] ; then
    sudo tee /etc/kafka/server.properties >/dev/null <<'KAFKA_PROPS_EOF'
# Generated by kafka-sbom.pcs from bisos.kafka.kafkaInfo. Written only when absent.
process.roles=broker,controller
node.id=1
controller.quorum.bootstrap.servers=127.0.0.1:24008
listeners=PLAINTEXT://127.0.0.1:24007,CONTROLLER://127.0.0.1:24008
advertised.listeners=PLAINTEXT://127.0.0.1:24007,CONTROLLER://127.0.0.1:24008
controller.listener.names=CONTROLLER
inter.broker.listener.name=PLAINTEXT
listener.security.protocol.map=CONTROLLER:PLAINTEXT,PLAINTEXT:PLAINTEXT
log.dirs=/var/lib/kafka/data
num.partitions=1
offsets.topic.replication.factor=1
transaction.state.log.replication.factor=1
transaction.state.log.min.isr=1
share.coordinator.state.topic.replication.factor=1
share.coordinator.state.topic.min.isr=1
log.retention.hours=168
KAFKA_PROPS_EOF
fi
sudo chown root:kafka /etc/kafka/server.properties
sudo chmod 640 /etc/kafka/server.properties

if [ ! -f /etc/default/kafka ] ; then
    sudo tee /etc/default/kafka >/dev/null <<'KAFKA_ENV_EOF'
KAFKA_HEAP_OPTS="-Xms512m -Xmx1g"
LOG_DIR=/var/log/kafka
# Add any overrides (e.g. KAFKA_JVM_PERFORMANCE_OPTS) here
KAFKA_ENV_EOF
fi
sudo chown root:kafka /etc/default/kafka
sudo chmod 640 /etc/default/kafka

# dataDir is kafka-owned 750: the invoker cannot see into it, so the test is run as root.
if ! sudo test -f /var/lib/kafka/data/meta.properties ; then
    clusterId=$(sudo -u kafka env LOG_DIR=/var/log/kafka \
        /opt/kafka/current/bin/kafka-storage.sh random-uuid)
    sudo -u kafka env LOG_DIR=/var/log/kafka \
        /opt/kafka/current/bin/kafka-storage.sh format --standalone \
        -t "$clusterId" -c /etc/kafka/server.properties
fi

set -e
sudo mkdir -p /opt/kafka/kafbat
if [ ! -f /opt/kafka/kafbat/api-v1.5.0.jar ] ; then
    tmpDir=$(mktemp -d)
    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpDir"' EXIT
    curl -fsSL -o "$tmpDir/kafbat.jar" https://github.com/kafbat/kafka-ui/releases/download/v1.5.0/api-v1.5.0.jar
    echo "8bebff7b21ddb084b5b647e271136f7d97f46da6c7bc70f9cd47775dfbd3c10e  $tmpDir/kafbat.jar" | sha256sum -c --quiet -
    sudo install -o root -g root -m 644 "$tmpDir/kafbat.jar" /opt/kafka/kafbat/api-v1.5.0.jar
fi

if [ ! -f /etc/default/kafbat-ui ] ; then
    sudo tee /etc/default/kafbat-ui >/dev/null <<'KAFBAT_ENV_EOF'
SERVER_ADDRESS=127.0.0.1
SERVER_PORT=24009
KAFKA_CLUSTERS_0_NAME=local
KAFKA_CLUSTERS_0_BOOTSTRAPSERVERS=127.0.0.1:24007
DYNAMIC_CONFIG_ENABLED=false
# Add any overrides (e.g. AUTH_TYPE, LOGGING_LEVEL_ROOT) here
KAFBAT_ENV_EOF
fi
sudo chown root:kafka /etc/default/kafbat-ui
sudo chmod 640 /etc/default/kafbat-ui

** cmnd= apt list librdkafkaSetup --installed  2> /dev/null

** cmnd= 
set -e
sudo mkdir -p /opt/kafka
if [ ! -f /opt/kafka/librdkafka-2.16.0/lib/librdkafka.so.1 ] ; then
    tmpDir=$(mktemp -d)
    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpDir"' EXIT
    curl -fsSL -o "$tmpDir/librdkafka.tgz" https://github.com/confluentinc/librdkafka/archive/refs/tags/v2.16.0.tar.gz
    echo "e6b61de61d3282879a88e4ee3d9f634a8b05bf78a9198dfc25c4820c0c9ed231  $tmpDir/librdkafka.tgz" | sha256sum -c --quiet -
    tar -xzf "$tmpDir/librdkafka.tgz" -C "$tmpDir"
    ( cd "$tmpDir/librdkafka-2.16.0" \
        && ./configure --prefix=/opt/kafka/librdkafka-2.16.0 --enable-gssapi >/dev/null \
        && make -j"$(nproc)" >/dev/null \
        && sudo make install >/dev/null )
fi
grep -q libsasl2 /opt/kafka/librdkafka-2.16.0/lib/pkgconfig/rdkafka.pc \
    || { echo "librdkafka was built without libsasl2: no Kerberos" >&2; exit 1; }

sudo ln -sfn /opt/kafka/librdkafka-2.16.0 /opt/kafka/librdkafka
sudo tee /opt/kafka/librdkafka-2.16.0/build-env.sh >/dev/null <<'RDK_ENV_EOF'
# Generated by kafka-sbom.pcs from bisos.kafka.kafkaInfo.
# Use:  . /opt/kafka/librdkafka/build-env.sh
#       pip install --no-binary confluent-kafka confluent-kafka==2.16.0
export C_INCLUDE_PATH=/opt/kafka/librdkafka/include${C_INCLUDE_PATH:+:$C_INCLUDE_PATH}
export LIBRARY_PATH=/opt/kafka/librdkafka/lib${LIBRARY_PATH:+:$LIBRARY_PATH}
export LDFLAGS="-Wl,-rpath,/opt/kafka/librdkafka/lib ${LDFLAGS:-}"
export PIP_NO_BINARY=confluent-kafka

[Unit]
Description=Apache Kafka (KRaft broker+controller)
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=kafka
Group=kafka
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/kafka

[Unit]
Description=Kafbat UI (web UI for Apache Kafka)
After=network.target kafka.service
Wants=kafka.service

[Service]
Type=simple
User=kafka
Group=kafka
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/kafbat-ui
WorkingDirectory=/opt/kafka/kafbat

SuccessExitStatus=143
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]

 seedtype=sysdSysUnit

[Unit]
Description=Grafana
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=grafana
Group=grafana
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/grafana
WorkingDirectory=/opt/grafana/current
ExecStart=/opt/grafana/current/bin/grafana server --homepath=/opt/grafana/current --config=/etc/grafana/grafana.ini
LimitNOFILE=10000
TimeoutStopSec=30
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
 seedtype=sysdSysUnit
** cmnd= sudo systemctl daemon-reload
** cmnd= sudo systemctl restart grafana.service
** cmnd= sudo systemctl enable grafana.service

set -e

# banna sysAccts: pypiserver uid=506, group pypiserver gid=506
if getent group pypiserver >/dev/null ; then
    liveGid=$(getent group pypiserver | cut -d: -f3)
    [ "$liveGid" = "506" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: group pypiserver has gid $liveGid, banna assigns 506 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent group 506 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: gid 506 (registered for group pypiserver) is held by group $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo groupadd -r -g 506 pypiserver
fi
if getent passwd pypiserver >/dev/null ; then
    liveUid=$(id -u pypiserver)
    [ "$liveUid" = "506" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: account pypiserver has uid $liveUid, banna assigns 506 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent passwd 506 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: uid 506 (registered for pypiserver) is held by account $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo useradd -r -u 506 -g pypiserver -M -d /var/lib/pypiserver \
        -s /usr/sbin/nologin -c 'pypiserver and pypi-browser (bisos.pypiserver)' pypiserver
fi


sudo mkdir -p /var/lib/pypiserver/packages /var/lib/pypiserver/browser-cache /etc/pypiserver
sudo chown -R pypiserver:pypiserver /var/lib/pypiserver
sudo chmod 750 /var/lib/pypiserver

/bisos/venv/py3/asc/bin/python - "$report" <<'PY'
import importlib.metadata as md, json, re, sys
owned = {"pypiserver","passlib","waitress"}
canon = lambda n: re.sub(r"[-_.]+", "-", n).lower()
changes = []
for item in json.load(open(sys.argv[1])).get("install", []):
    name, version = item["metadata"]["name"], item["metadata"]["version"]
    try:
        current = md.version(name)
    except md.PackageNotFoundError:
        continue
    if current != version and canon(name) not in owned:
        changes.append(f"{name} {current} -> {version}")
if changes:
    print("refused: the pins would change packages other capabilities installed in the venv:", file=sys.stderr)
    for c in changes:
        print("  " + c, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)
PY

# Add flags to ARGS above. No PyPI mirror (root/pypi): --no-root-pypi at init.

fi
sudo chown root:devpi /etc/default/devpi
sudo chmod 640 /etc/default/devpi

if ! sudo test -f /var/lib/devpi/server/.serverversion ; then
    sudo -u devpi /bisos/venv/py3/isolated/devpiServer/bin/devpi-init --serverdir /var/lib/devpi/server \
        --no-root-pypi --root-passwd devpi
fi

sudo tee /etc/devpi/devpi-accounts.py >/dev/null <<'DEVPI_ACCTS_EOF'
import base64, json, sys, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
# Generated by devpi-sbom.pcs from bisos.devpi.devpiInfo. Rewritten on every sbom run.
BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:24015"


def call(method, path, body=None, auth=None):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, method=method,
                                 data=None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(),
                                 headers={"Accept": "application/json",
                                          "Content-Type": "application/json"})
    if auth:
        req.add_header("X-Devpi-Auth", base64.b64encode(("%s:%s" % auth).encode()).decode())
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as r:
            return r.status, json.load(r)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, None


def login(user, password):
    status, body = call("POST", "/+login", {"user": user, "password": password})
    if status != 200:
        sys.exit("devpi-accounts: login %s failed: %s" % (user, status))
    return (user, body["result"]["password"])


for _ in range(60):
    try:
        urllib.request.urlopen(BASE + "/+api", timeout=5)
        break
    except Exception:
        time.sleep(1)
else:
    sys.exit("devpi-accounts: devpi-server did not answer at " + BASE)

root = login("root", "devpi")
status, _ = call("PUT", "/admin", {"password": "devpi"}, root)
if status not in (200, 201, 409):
    sys.exit("devpi-accounts: creating user admin: %s" % status)
admin = login("admin", "devpi")
status, _ = call("GET", "/admin/dev")
if status == 404:
    status, _ = call("PUT", "/admin/dev", {"type": "stage", "bases": [], "volatile": True}, admin)
    if status not in (200, 201):
        sys.exit("devpi-accounts: creating index admin/dev: %s" % status)
print("devpi-accounts: admin and admin/dev present")
DEVPI_ACCTS_EOF
sudo chown root:devpi /etc/devpi /etc/devpi/devpi-accounts.py
sudo chmod 750 /etc/devpi
sudo chmod 640 /etc/devpi/devpi-accounts.py

[Unit]
Description=devpi-server (Python package index, with devpi-web)

[Service]
Type=simple

Group=devpi
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/devpi
ExecStart=/bisos/venv/py3/isolated/devpiServer/bin/devpi-server $ARGS
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]

** cmnd= 
set -e

# banna sysAccts: geneweb uid=508, group geneweb gid=508
if getent group geneweb >/dev/null ; then
    liveGid=$(getent group geneweb | cut -d: -f3)
    [ "$liveGid" = "508" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: group geneweb has gid $liveGid, banna assigns 508 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent group 508 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: gid 508 (registered for group geneweb) is held by group $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo groupadd -r -g 508 geneweb
fi
if getent passwd geneweb >/dev/null ; then
    liveUid=$(id -u geneweb)
    [ "$liveUid" = "508" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: account geneweb has uid $liveUid, banna assigns 508 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent passwd 508 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: uid 508 (registered for geneweb) is held by account $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo useradd -r -u 508 -g geneweb -M -d /var/lib/geneweb \
        -s /usr/sbin/nologin -c 'GeneWeb gwd (bisos.geneweb)' geneweb
fi


sudo mkdir -p /var/lib/geneweb/bases /var/lib/geneweb/wd /opt/geneweb
sudo chown -R geneweb:geneweb /var/lib/geneweb
sudo chmod 750 /var/lib/geneweb

set -e

case "$arch" in

dir=/opt/geneweb/geneweb-7.1.0-beta2
sudo mkdir -p /opt/geneweb
if [ ! -d "$dir" ] ; then
    tmpDir=$(mktemp -d)
    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpDir"' EXIT
    curl -fsSL -o "$tmpDir/dist.zip" https://github.com/geneweb/geneweb/releases/download/v7.1.0-beta2/geneweb-7.1.0-beta2-linux.zip
    echo "$sha  $tmpDir/dist.zip" | sha256sum -c --quiet -
    sudo mkdir -p "$dir"
    sudo unzip -q "$tmpDir/dist.zip" -d "$dir"
    sudo chown -R root:root "$dir"
fi
sudo ln -sfn "$dir" /opt/geneweb/current

if [ ! -f /etc/default/geneweb ] ; then
    sudo tee /etc/default/geneweb >/dev/null <<'GENEWEB_ENV_EOF'
ARGS="-hd /opt/geneweb/current/gw -bd /var/lib/geneweb/bases -wd /var/lib/geneweb/wd -a 127.0.0.1 -p 24017 -n_workers 2 -lang en -wizard geneweb -log -"
# Add gwd flags (e.g. -friend <passwd>, -robot_xcl 10,60) to ARGS above
GENEWEB_ENV_EOF
fi
sudo chown root:geneweb /etc/default/geneweb
sudo chmod 640 /etc/default/geneweb

if ! sudo test -d /var/lib/geneweb/bases/demo.gwb ; then
    sudo -u geneweb sh -c 'cd /var/lib/geneweb/bases && /opt/geneweb/current/gw/gwc -o demo'
fi

asRootWrite: destPath=/etc/systemd/system/geneweb.service -- contentStr=
[Unit]
Description=GeneWeb gwd (genealogy web server)
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=geneweb
Group=geneweb
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/geneweb
WorkingDirectory=/var/lib/geneweb/bases
ExecStart=/opt/geneweb/current/gw/gwd $ARGS
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
 seedtype=sysdSysUnit
** cmnd= sudo systemctl daemon-reload
** cmnd= sudo systemctl restart geneweb.service
** cmnd= sudo systemctl enable geneweb.service

** cmnd= apt list csPlayerPerfAcctSetup --installed  2> /dev/null
Listing...
** cmnd= 
set -e

# banna sysAccts: csplayer uid=509, group csplayer gid=509
if getent group csplayer >/dev/null ; then
    liveGid=$(getent group csplayer | cut -d: -f3)
    [ "$liveGid" = "509" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: group csplayer has gid $liveGid, banna assigns 509 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent group 509 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: gid 509 (registered for group csplayer) is held by group $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo groupadd -r -g 509 csplayer
fi
if getent passwd csplayer >/dev/null ; then
    liveUid=$(id -u csplayer)
    [ "$liveUid" = "509" ] || \
        echo "banna: WARNING: account csplayer has uid $liveUid, banna assigns 509 -- see bannaInfo.cs -i bannaSysAcctsVerify" >&2
else
    holder=$(getent passwd 509 | cut -d: -f1)
    if [ -n "$holder" ] ; then
        echo "banna: ERROR: uid 509 (registered for csplayer) is held by account $holder" >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sudo useradd -r -u 509 -g csplayer -M -d /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf \
        -s /usr/sbin/nologin -c 'csPlayer performer, its Django REST API and operations (csPlayerPerf-drf)' csplayer
fi

sudo mkdir -p /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/static /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/data /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/operationTasks
sudo chown csplayer:csplayer /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/static /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/data /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/operationTasks
sudo chmod 755 /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/static
sudo chmod 750 /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/data /bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/operationTasks

set -e

# csPlayerPerf (bisos-web/csPlayerPerf-drf): read by its unit and by cbm/csPlayerPerf-manage.sh.
# Written once by cbm/csPlayerPerf-sbom.pcs; edits survive re-runs.
DJANGO_SECRET_KEY=8I2ahwJGACqAuXC7zE2PCW2nfy5Uw-gTIvrzEEjHfbtc-XWSfp5EiI2H2ejfAJVbd2A
DEBUG_FLAG=False
CSPLAYER_ALLOWED_HOSTS=csPlayerUi.internal,127.0.0.1,localhost
CSPLAYER_CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS=http://csPlayerUi.internal
CSPLAYER_DB_PATH=/bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/data/db.sqlite3
CSPLAYER_STATIC_ROOT=/bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/static
CSPLAYER_OPERATIONS_DIR=/bisos/var/csPlayerPerf/operationTasks
CSPLAYERPERF_ENV_EOF
fi
sudo chown root:csplayer /etc/default/csPlayerPerf
sudo chmod 640 /etc/default/csPlayerPerf

Action ensure
** cmnd= /bisos/git/bxRepos/bisos-web/csPlayerPerf-drf/cbm/csPlayerPerf-sysd.pcs  --cls="configFile_sysdUnit" --runAs="root"  -i configFileUpdate
asRootWrite: destPath=/etc/systemd/system/csPlayerPerf.service -- contentStr=
[Unit]
Description=csPlayerPerf --- csPlayer's performer (Django REST API, gunicorn)
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=csplayer
Group=csplayer
EnvironmentFile=/etc/default/csPlayerPerf
WorkingDirectory=/bisos/git/bxRepos/bisos-web/csPlayerPerf-drf/server
ExecStart=/bisos/venv/py3/asc/bin/gunicorn csPlayerPerf.wsgi:application --bind 127.0.0.1:23001 --workers 1 --threads 8 --access-logfile -
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
 seedtype=sysdSysUnit
** cmnd= sudo systemctl daemon-reload
** cmnd= sudo systemctl restart csPlayerPerf.service
** cmnd= sudo systemctl enable csPlayerPerf.service
